## [0.1.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/releases/tag/cf-gears-license-enforcement-sdk-v0.1.0) - 2026-10-30

### Added

- *(license-enforcement)* remove unnecessary deny_unknown_fields attribute (by @nonameffh)
- *(license-enforcement)* specify canonical error mapping for admission conflict errors (by @nonameffh)
- *(license-enforcement)* implement SDK (by @nonameffh)

### Fixed

- *(license-enforcement)* resolve code review findings (by @nonameffh)
- *(license-enforcement)* fix lint (by @nonameffh)
- *(license-enforcement)* fix SDK and evidence fields (by @nonameffh)

### Contributors

* @nonameffh

## [0.2.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/compare/cf-gears-llm-gateway-sdk-v0.1.13...cf-gears-llm-gateway-sdk-v0.2.0) - 2026-10-30

### Added

- *(llm-gateway)* add allowed_domains to the web search tool (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* add a cancellation token to the provider call context (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* [**breaking**] align llm-gateway-sdk with the PRD (by @Ritmix3300)

### Fixed

- *(llm-gateway)* [**breaking**] make new public SDK types non_exhaustive with constructors (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* make ProviderErrorCategory non_exhaustive (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* truncate provider text in wire errors (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* [**breaking**] drop stream from the SDK request body (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* make the media_not_found message fixed text (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* drop the P2 timeout error from the SDK (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* add permission_denied and gateway_unavailable errors (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* restore output_validation_error as a distinct code (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* keep web search tool options instead of falling through to Other (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* use cf_gears: prefix for web search types (by @Ritmix3300)

### Other

- *(llm-gateway)* drop stale PRD deviation notes from the SDK (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* describe the key destination as the OAGW upstream alias (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* describe unapproved_destination as an unresolved OAGW alias (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* drop the serde-only web search roundtrip test (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* cover ResponseResource gateway extensions (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* list all sources of ignored_parameters (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* clarify where unsupported_schema is checked (by @Ritmix3300)
- *(llm-gateway)* [**breaking**] drop model_support from the plugin SDK (by @Ritmix3300)

### Contributors

## [0.1.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/releases/tag/cf-gears-construct-v0.1.0) - 2026-10-28

### Added

- *(construct)* add the planner evaluation set
- *(construct)* store plan values as JSON and check their text pieces
- *(construct)* bring the fact candidate to its current shape
- *(gdl)* introduce GDL, the description language for gears (by @MikeFalcon77)
- *(construct)* add the manage-facts operation
- *(construct)* process records from a queue
- *(construct)* add the planner prompt
- *(construct)* add the planner agent loop
- *(construct)* add the planner core
- *(construct)* add the model client
- *(construct)* add sensitive-data checks and admission
- *(construct)* keep the record's observed_at in the envelope
- *(construct)* drop received records with an event until the planner exists
- *(construct)* add record intake
- *(construct)* add the subject's settings routes
- *(construct)* add subject settings storage and read
- *(construct)* add tracing spans to the note operations
- *(construct)* add the gear foundation shell

### Fixed

- *(construct)* build again after the record types and manage-facts merges
- *(construct)* write one admission event per value and kind
- *(construct)* block a piece one redacting kind names twice, once without a range
- *(construct)* apply the local review of the checks and admission
- *(construct)* apply the review of admission's verdict enforcement
- *(construct)* cap a plan value as a whole, not each text piece
- *(construct)* apply the local review of the fact candidate rules
- *(construct)* apply the local review of the record key and types
- *(construct)* keep and log a record identity only as its key
- *(construct)* apply the CodeRabbit review of the record intake stack
- *(construct)* refuse out-of-scope settings writes like reads
- *(construct)* refuse out-of-scope settings reads with their own error
- *(construct)* name the settings resource by its GTS type and its owner
- *(construct)* apply the CodeRabbit review of record intake
- *(construct)* pin utf8mb4 for the MySQL record identity table
- *(construct)* apply the review of the subject's settings routes
- *(construct)* pin utf8mb4 for the MySQL notes table
- *(construct)* keep the retry meaning of a policy-service failure, reject unknown request fields
- *(construct)* report a failing policy service as unavailable, add tests
- *(construct)* return RFC 9457 problems for malformed request bodies
- *(construct)* follow the database object-namespacing ADR
- *(construct)* apply review findings to the gear foundation shell

### Other

- Merge pull request #5311 from tob71/ROL-17377-sensitive-data-checks-admission (by @Artifizer) - #5311
- Merge pull request #5385 from nikbackdev/construct-manage-facts (by @Artifizer) - #5385
- *(construct)* drop planning and tracker references from the checks and admission
- *(construct)* drop planning and tracker references from record intake
- *(construct)* name the broken-constraint case type
- *(construct)* define the record types as Rust structs
- *(construct)* drop the first-feature claim, test the migration runs twice
- *(toolkit-oop)* Profile 2 is Self-Hosted, its host is Flight Control (by @MikeFalcon77)
- *(construct)* state that the hand-off carries no security context
- *(construct)* let record intake name the tenant
- *(construct)* log settings read errors once, where they are mapped
- *(construct)* log each failure once and simplify the retry check
- *(construct)* cover the policy-service failure paths and the 403 body
- *(construct)* add @cpt traceability markers to the gear foundation

* @Artifizer
* @MikeFalcon77

### Added

- *(gdl)* introduce GDL, the description language for gears (by @MikeFalcon77)

- *(quota-enforcement)* quota lifecycle, metadata validation and lifecycle gauges (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* projection contract catalogue and subject attribution (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* register the gear and its storage plugin in the example server (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* REST surface and healthcheck (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* bootstrap, readiness and the PDP reachability probe (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* plugin resolution, admission and the enforcement point (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* sweeper election on the cluster gear (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* domain output ports and the metrics meter (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* gear configuration and canonical error mapping (by @vgprod)

- *(quota-enforcement)* mark the lifecycle domain types with domain_model (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* mark domain types with domain_model, scope the catalogue compatibility step to Quotas, document helpers (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* build GTS ids with gts_id! and document the canonical resources (by @vgprod)

- *(quota-enforcement)* keep the bootstrap error out of /health (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* path-only cluster dev-deps (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* bound write-path policy validation and cover denial, cache and determinism (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* bound plugin selection and test the coordinator stop timeout (by @vgprod)
- *(quota-enforcement)* bound the admission PDP call with pdp_deadline_ms (by @vgprod)

- *(github-mirror)* recheck the rate-limit cooldown after taking a request slot (by @AndIConstructor)

* @modcrafter77

* @mattgarmon

### Added

- `toolkit-auth`: shared asynchronous OAuth2 acquisition, explicit shutdown and
  typed HTTP failures with sanitized target URLs.

### Breaking

- `toolkit-auth` (0.8.0): rename `Token` to `TokenProvider`; construction becomes
  synchronous and makes no network requests. Replace `new(config).await?` with
  `TokenProvider::new(config)?`, then call `get().await?` when an initial token is
  needed. `get()` is now async.
  `invalidate().await` now returns `Result<(), TokenError>` and immediately clears
  the old credential, sharing replacement across callers. `shutdown().await`
  stops all clones and joins the worker. See [migration notes](#toolkit-auth-080-migration).
- `toolkit-auth`: rejected HTTP responses now return non-exhaustive
  `TokenError::HttpStatus { operation, status, target_url, .. }` instead of
  `TokenError::Http(String)`. Match both variants or use `TokenError::status()`;
  transport failures remain `Http`. URLs omit userinfo, query and fragment.
- `toolkit`: an out-of-process gear serving `.authenticated()` operations without a
  tenant authenticator refuses to start. Link `authn-resolver` into the worker, or set
  the new development opt-out `oop_http.auth_disabled: true` (non-platform routes then
  run with the default tenant context; platform routes still need `internal_auth`).
- `toolkit`: `OopHttpConfig` and `toolkit::runtime::OopServeOptions` gain the public
  field `auth_disabled`. Configuration files are unaffected (it defaults to `false`);
  code constructing either struct with a literal must set it
  (`OopServeOptions::with_auth_disabled` sets it on the builder).
- `toolkit-http-middleware`: `layer_route_auth(.., None, ..)` without a bearer
  authenticator now fails closed (`TenantPlane::Absent`): tenant and unlisted routes
  answer 401 `TENANT_AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED` instead of reaching the handler, and a request
  no route matches is 404 without running the router's fallback. Pass a bearer
  authenticator, or call `layer_route_auth_with(.., TenantPlane::Disabled, ..)` for the
  development default tenant context.
- `toolkit-http-middleware`: `security_context_middleware` ignores a bearer on an
  `AnonymousRoute` instead of validating it: the handler keeps the anonymous context, and
  an invalid bearer there is no longer 401.

#### toolkit-auth 0.8.0 migration

- Replace `Token` imports and type annotations with `TokenProvider`.
- Replace `Token::new(config).await?` with `TokenProvider::new(config)?`.
  Construction no longer contacts the identity provider. If startup requires a
  token, call `token_provider.get().await?` after construction.
- Replace synchronous `get()` calls with `get().await`.
- Handle the result of `invalidate().await`; it returns `Result<(), TokenError>`.
  It clears the old credential before replacement. Failure does not restore it.
- Call `shutdown().await` when the owning application stops. This stops all
  clones, including those retained by HTTP clients, and joins the worker.
- Update error handling: HTTP response failures use `TokenError::HttpStatus`
  instead of `TokenError::Http(String)`. Existing matches on `Http(..)` still
  compile but no longer catch response failures. Match both variants or use
  `TokenError::status()` when classification depends on the HTTP status.

### Changed

- A bearer on an `.anonymous()` route is ignored on out-of-process listeners as on the
  gateway: the handler gets the anonymous context, and a forged bearer is no longer 401.
- An out-of-process listener without a tenant authenticator answers tenant and
  undeclared routes with 401 `TENANT_AUTH_NOT_CONFIGURED` instead of reaching the
  handler unauthenticated; a path nothing serves keeps its 404, and a method undeclared
  on a declared path its 405.
  Platform operations without `oop_http.internal_auth` warn at startup and answer 401,
  as on the gateway.

### Fixed

- Resource-group filters accept quoted UUIDs and resolve membership GTS type paths
  to storage IDs while preserving exact registered identifiers, including legacy
  codes accepted by type creation outside the stricter GTS grammar. Invalid
  query parameters return HTTP 400 instead of 500. GTS filters support `eq`, `ne`,
  and `in`; operators without meaningful surrogate-ID semantics are rejected.

### Fixed

- Documented that OoP HTTP bootstrap `oop_http.allow_loopback_advertise` **defaults to `false`**. A loopback
  or unspecified `advertise_uri` (`127.0.0.1`, `::1`, `localhost`, `0.0.0.0`, `[::]`) — including the value
  derived from an unspecified `listen_addr` when `advertise_uri` is unset — refuses to boot. Set a routable
  `advertise_uri`, or `oop_http.allow_loopback_advertise: true` for single-host / local-dev. (#4581)

## [0.1.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/releases/tag/cf-chat-engine-v0.1.0) - 2026-07-23

### Fixed

- *(chat-engine)* add missing README.md and guard against missing packaging metadata (by @MikeFalcon77)
- fix cargo fmt
- fix dylint
- fix cargo fmt
- fix clippy

### Other

- Merge pull request #4251 from MikeFalcon77/fix/missed_README (by @MikeFalcon77) - #4251
- *(deps)* migrate to gts-rust v0.11.0 (by @aviator5)
- drop unused inventory/async-stream deps
- enforce domain/infra boundary + quick-xml advisory
- author missing ADRs/entities and clear dylint lints
- terse o/p/v delta keys + stop terminator (FR-024)
- persist streamed vocabulary events
- expand streaming event vocabulary
- typed SSE delta events
- drop dead NDJSON response path
- sweep the resume buffer on a TTL cadence
- add Last-Event-ID reconnect endpoint
- driver outlives the client connection
- tee streaming events into resume buffer
- SSE buffer-reader for live-tail resume
- stream resume buffer storage
- SSE delta transport for streaming endpoints
- delta-stream projector
- hard-delete cascade + summary variant-index collision
- canonicalize inbound text part content to {"text": ...}
- mount /session-types + fix streaming cancel-on-drop
- wire messaging REST handlers + fix message-id route arity
- persist on_session_updated metadata on type-switch
- session hooks return capabilities + metadata
- per-part citations & references
- structured message parts
- add user_id and tenant_id to message
- enforce root-message variant uniqueness
- remove always-failing VariantService::assign_variant_index
- report session-creation rollback failures explicitly
- persist plugin_config at session-type registration
- don't log plugin error payload in health-check warn
- emit summary Complete only after successful persist
- drop constructor-echo tautology tests in message_repo
- model messages.role as enum
- bound retention session scan with limit
- batch delete_message_subtree by level
- gate retention cleanup behind leader election
- gate stubbed export/search behind HTTP 501
- OData session pagination + port modkit→toolkit
- move chat engine to gears

### Contributors

* @MikeFalcon77
* @aviator5

## [0.1.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/releases/tag/cf-chat-engine-v0.1.0) - 2026-07-23

### Fixed

- *(security)* type credential fields as SecretString; close review gaps (by @MikeFalcon77)
- fix cargo fmt
- fix dylint
- fix cargo fmt
- fix clippy

### Other

- *(deps)* migrate to gts-rust v0.11.0 (by @aviator5)
- drop unused inventory/async-stream deps
- enforce domain/infra boundary + quick-xml advisory
- author missing ADRs/entities and clear dylint lints
- terse o/p/v delta keys + stop terminator (FR-024)
- persist streamed vocabulary events
- expand streaming event vocabulary
- typed SSE delta events
- drop dead NDJSON response path
- sweep the resume buffer on a TTL cadence
- add Last-Event-ID reconnect endpoint
- driver outlives the client connection
- tee streaming events into resume buffer
- SSE buffer-reader for live-tail resume
- stream resume buffer storage
- SSE delta transport for streaming endpoints
- delta-stream projector
- hard-delete cascade + summary variant-index collision
- canonicalize inbound text part content to {"text": ...}
- mount /session-types + fix streaming cancel-on-drop
- wire messaging REST handlers + fix message-id route arity
- persist on_session_updated metadata on type-switch
- session hooks return capabilities + metadata
- per-part citations & references
- structured message parts
- add user_id and tenant_id to message
- enforce root-message variant uniqueness
- remove always-failing VariantService::assign_variant_index
- report session-creation rollback failures explicitly
- persist plugin_config at session-type registration
- don't log plugin error payload in health-check warn
- emit summary Complete only after successful persist
- drop constructor-echo tautology tests in message_repo
- model messages.role as enum
- bound retention session scan with limit
- batch delete_message_subtree by level
- gate retention cleanup behind leader election
- gate stubbed export/search behind HTTP 501
- OData session pagination + port modkit→toolkit
- move chat engine to gears

* @MikeFalcon77
* @aviator5

- *(deps)* migrate to gts-rust v0.11.0 (by @aviator5)
- drop unused inventory/async-stream deps
- enforce domain/infra boundary + quick-xml advisory
- author missing ADRs/entities and clear dylint lints
- terse o/p/v delta keys + stop terminator (FR-024)
- persist streamed vocabulary events
- expand streaming event vocabulary
- typed SSE delta events
- drop dead NDJSON response path
- sweep the resume buffer on a TTL cadence
- add Last-Event-ID reconnect endpoint
- driver outlives the client connection
- tee streaming events into resume buffer
- SSE buffer-reader for live-tail resume
- stream resume buffer storage
- SSE delta transport for streaming endpoints
- delta-stream projector
- hard-delete cascade + summary variant-index collision
- canonicalize inbound text part content to {"text": ...}
- mount /session-types + fix streaming cancel-on-drop
- wire messaging REST handlers + fix message-id route arity
- persist on_session_updated metadata on type-switch
- session hooks return capabilities + metadata
- per-part citations & references
- structured message parts
- add user_id and tenant_id to message
- enforce root-message variant uniqueness
- remove always-failing VariantService::assign_variant_index
- report session-creation rollback failures explicitly
- persist plugin_config at session-type registration
- don't log plugin error payload in health-check warn
- emit summary Complete only after successful persist
- drop constructor-echo tautology tests in message_repo
- model messages.role as enum
- bound retention session scan with limit
- batch delete_message_subtree by level
- gate retention cleanup behind leader election
- gate stubbed export/search behind HTTP 501
- OData session pagination + port modkit→toolkit
- move chat engine to gears

## [0.1.0](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/releases/tag/cf-chat-engine-v0.1.0) - 2026-07-22

### Fixed

- *(security)* type credential fields as SecretString; close review gaps (by @MikeFalcon77)
- fix cargo fmt
- fix dylint
- fix cargo fmt
- fix clippy

### Other

- *(deps)* migrate to gts-rust v0.11.0 (by @aviator5)
- drop unused inventory/async-stream deps
- enforce domain/infra boundary + quick-xml advisory
- author missing ADRs/entities and clear dylint lints
- terse o/p/v delta keys + stop terminator (FR-024)
- persist streamed vocabulary events
- expand streaming event vocabulary
- typed SSE delta events
- drop dead NDJSON response path
- sweep the resume buffer on a TTL cadence
- add Last-Event-ID reconnect endpoint
- driver outlives the client connection
- tee streaming events into resume buffer
- SSE buffer-reader for live-tail resume
- stream resume buffer storage
- SSE delta transport for streaming endpoints
- delta-stream projector
- hard-delete cascade + summary variant-index collision
- canonicalize inbound text part content to {"text": ...}
- mount /session-types + fix streaming cancel-on-drop
- wire messaging REST handlers + fix message-id route arity
- persist on_session_updated metadata on type-switch
- session hooks return capabilities + metadata
- per-part citations & references
- structured message parts
- add user_id and tenant_id to message
- enforce root-message variant uniqueness
- remove always-failing VariantService::assign_variant_index
- report session-creation rollback failures explicitly
- persist plugin_config at session-type registration
- don't log plugin error payload in health-check warn
- emit summary Complete only after successful persist
- drop constructor-echo tautology tests in message_repo
- model messages.role as enum
- bound retention session scan with limit
- batch delete_message_subtree by level
- gate retention cleanup behind leader election
- gate stubbed export/search behind HTTP 501
- OData session pagination + port modkit→toolkit
- move chat engine to gears

### Contributors

* @MikeFalcon77
* @aviator5
